18911:   function cfImageRole(srcP,isFacial,isIntraoral,isIntraoralDark){
18912:     if(isIntraoralDark) return 'contrastor_anterior_closeup';

18918:     const ortho=[],restor=[];
18919:     (pl||[]).forEach(f=>{
18920:       const t=String((f&&f.en)||f||''); if(!t) return;
18921:       if(CF_ORTHO_KEYS.test(t)) ortho.push(t);
18922:       else if(CF_RESTOR_KEYS.test(t)) restor.push(t);
18923:     });
18924:     return {ortho,restor,uncertain:[]};

18930:       const scope=state.simCorrectionScope||'combined';

18932:       const pl=(srcP&&Array.isArray(srcP.problemList))?srcP.problemList:[];

18938:               uncertain:(pc.uncertainVisibility||[]).map(String) };
18939:       } else cls=cfClassifyLegacy(pl);

18941:       parts.push('PRIORITY ORDER for this simulation: (1) the clinician\'s explicit request and slider settings, (2) clinically VISIBLE findings, (3) harmony of the face, lips, dentition and gingiva, (4) natural esthetic balance. Ideal ratios such as the golden proportion are SOFT reference values only — never rigid rules, and deviation alone is not an indication for correction.');
18942:       const role=cfImageRole(srcP,isFacial,isIntraoral,isIntraoralDark);
18943:       parts.push('IMAGE ROLE ('+role.replace(/_/g,' ')+'): weight the evaluation and the edit toward '+CF_ROLE_FOCUS[role]+'.');
18944:       if(cls.ortho.length) parts.push('ORTHODONTIC-PRIORITY findings ['+cls.ortho.slice(0,6).join('; ')+']: these must be expressed as tooth POSITION / axis / arch-relationship corrections. Do NOT mask or "solve" them with whitening, crown re-shaping or a veneer-like disguise alone.');
18945:       if(cls.restor.length) parts.push('RESTORATIVE-PRIORITY findings ['+cls.restor.slice(0,6).join('; ')+']: these must be expressed as crown FORM / SIZE / SHADE / restoration corrections. Do NOT present them as if solved purely by tooth movement.');
18946:       if(cls.uncertain.length) parts.push('LOW-VISIBILITY / UNCERTAIN items ['+cls.uncertain.slice(0,5).join('; ')+']: do NOT invent details or aggressively correct areas that are hidden by lips, shadow or low resolution — use a conservative strategy there.');
18947:       else parts.push('For any area hidden by the lips or unclear in this photo, use a conservative strategy — do not aggressively invent or correct what cannot be seen.');
18948:       /* confidence-based modulation */
18949:       if(sf&&typeof sf.confidence==='number'&&sf.confidence>0&&sf.confidence<0.55){
18950:         parts.push('Overall finding confidence is LOW ('+Math.round(sf.confidence*100)+'%): avoid strong corrections that rely on those findings; prefer the clinician\'s explicit request and the clearly visible structures.');
18951:       }
18952:       /* structured guidance passthrough */
18953:       if(sf&&sf.simulationGuidance){
18954:         const g=sf.simulationGuidance;

18956:         if(Array.isArray(g.avoid)&&g.avoid.length) parts.push('AVOID (from screening): '+g.avoid.slice(0,6).join('; ')+'.');
18957:       }
18958:       const custom=String(state.simCustomPrompt||'').trim();
18959:       if(mode==='user-first'){
18960:         parts.push('USER-FIRST MODE: the clinician\'s written request'+(custom?' ("'+custom.slice(0,180)+'")':'')+' and slider settings define the ONLY intended changes. Apply automated findings ONLY where they directly support that request. Add NO corrections beyond it — items the clinician chose to keep (e.g. a peg lateral to preserve, residual character to retain, brackets to keep) must be kept exactly.');
18961:       } else if(mode==='auto'){
18962:         parts.push('AUTO MODE: where the clinician has not specified details, the automated findings may guide the correction — still within the treatment concept, slider strengths and all constraints above.');
18963:       } else {
18964:         parts.push('BALANCED MODE: reconcile the clinician\'s request with the visible findings. Where they conflict, the clinician\'s request always wins; unrequested corrections stay minimal.');
18965:       }
18966:       if(minimal) parts.push('MINIMAL-CHANGE MODE (strict): change ONLY what the request, sliders and concept clearly imply, at the smallest believable magnitude. Explicitly FORBIDDEN: unrequested whitening; unrequested reshaping or idealisation; excessive uniformity of the teeth; lip, skin or facial beautification; and any large cosmetic change the clinician did not ask for.');

18973:       };

The final result must remain natural, biologically plausible, clinically believable, patient-specific and identity-preserving.');

18981:   function buildClinicalPrompt(){
18982:     const v=state.simV82||{}, concept=v.concept||'ortho', s=state.simV96||{};
18983:     const conceptMap={ortho:'orthodontic alignment only (no veneers or crowns)',prosthetic:'prosthetic restoration with veneers/crowns',hybrid:'combined orthodontic and prosthetic treatment'};
18984:     const srcP = state._simSource||pickSource();
18985:     const kind=(typeof sourceKind==='function') ? sourceKind(srcP) : 'Image';
18986:     const isFacial = (kind==='Facial');
18987:     const isIntraoral = !!(srcP && srcP.intraoral);
18988:     const isIntraoralDark = !!(srcP && srcP.intraoralDark);   // black-contraster subtype
18989:     // Whether lips / perioral soft tissue are present in the photo. Drives whether the model
18990:     // is allowed to render/arrange lips at all. Default: only a facial portrait has lips.
18991:     const hasLips = (srcP && typeof srcP.lips==='boolean') ? srcP.lips : (isFacial && !isIntraoral);
18992:     // describe slider intensity in words so low values stay barely perceptible
18993:     const lvl=(x)=>{ x=x||0; if(x<15) return null; if(x<40) return 'very subtle'; if(x<70) return 'moderate, still natural'; return 'noticeable but realistic'; };
18994: 
18995:     // ===== Intraoral close-up: a SHORT, teeth-only prompt =====
18996:     // A long prompt full of "smile arc / lip line / smile frame / buccal corridor" wording
18997:     // makes the img2img model hallucinate a brand-new smiling face instead of editing the
18998:     // teeth in the supplied close-up. For intraoral shots we therefore emit a tight,
18999:     // edit-this-image-only instruction and explicitly forbid creating a smile / lips / face.
19000:     if(isIntraoral){
19001:       const ip=[];
19002:       // Orthodontic / ortho+prosthetic intent on a retracted (both-arches) smile-focus
19003:       // view: the goal is to IDEALLY align the upper arch and build the occlusion, so we
19004:       // must allow teeth to be repositioned (the strict "freeze every incisal edge" rule
19005:       // is only kept for non-ortho concepts and the single-arch black-contraster shot).
19006:       const orthoGoal = (concept==='ortho' || concept==='hybrid');
19007:       // Slider MAX => extraction-based orthodontics: the most aggressive plan,
19008:       // allowed to remove premolars to fully resolve severe crowding.
19009:       const extraction = orthoGoal && !isIntraoralDark && Math.round(s.alignment)>=100;
19010:       const countRule = extraction
19011:         ? 'as part of an EXTRACTION orthodontic plan you MAY remove one tooth per quadrant (typically the first premolars) to create space, then close the spaces — but do NOT add any new teeth'
19012:         : 'keep the same tooth count';
19013:       ip.push('Edit ONLY the teeth in THIS exact photograph (image-to-image). Do not generate a new picture.');
19014:       ip.push('The photo is a close-up INTRAORAL view taken with cheek retractors. It is NOT a smiling face — do NOT turn it into a smile, do NOT add lips, do NOT add a face or skin, do NOT add new teeth, '+(extraction?'':'do NOT remove teeth, ')+'and do NOT change the framing, magnification, background or gingiva.');
19015:       if(orthoGoal && !isIntraoralDark){
19016:         ip.push('TOP PRIORITY — alignment is allowed: you MAY reposition, de-rotate, tip and level the existing teeth to align the arches, but NEVER make any individual tooth unnaturally long and NEVER paint new tooth structure below the lowest existing incisal edges into the background. Keep realistic crown proportions; '+countRule+'.');

19553:   // ── Pixel heuristic — 4-step local analysis ───────────────────────────────────
19554:   // Step 3 (Occlusion — deep bite / open bite / crossbite) cannot be reliably inferred
19555:   // from frontal-view pixel statistics and is therefore omitted in this tier. All other
19556:   // steps are covered. colMaxY[x] = lowest tooth pixel per column = incisal edge position
19557:   // (Y increases downward; incisal edges are at the bottom of a frontal smile).
19558:   async function buildProblemListPixel(dataUrl){
19559:     try{
19560:       const img=await loadImage(dataUrl);
19561:       const iw=img.naturalWidth||img.width, ih=img.naturalHeight||img.height;
19562:       const W=256, H=Math.max(8,Math.round(W*ih/iw));
19563:       const c=document.createElement('canvas'); c.width=W; c.height=H;
19564:       const ctx=c.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true}); ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,W,H);
19565:       const band=detectBand(ctx,W,H);
19566:       const mask=buildToothMask(ctx,W,H,band);
19567:       if(!mask) return [];
19568:       let d; try{ d=ctx.getImageData(0,0,W,H).data; }catch(e){ return []; }
19569:       let n=0,sL=0,sC=0,minX=W,maxX=0,minY=H,maxY=0,leftN=0,rightN=0;
19570:       const colMaxY=new Int16Array(W).fill(-1);
19571:       const bandCx=(band.x0+band.x1)/2;
19572:       for(let q=0,p=0;q<mask.length;q++,p+=4){ if(mask[q]<0.5) continue;
19573:         const r=d[p],g=d[p+1],b=d[p+2],l=(r+g+b)/3,ch=Math.max(r,g,b)-Math.min(r,g,b);
19574:         n++; sL+=l; sC+=ch;
19575:         const x=q%W,y=(q/W)|0;
19576:         if(x<minX)minX=x; if(x>maxX)maxX=x; if(y<minY)minY=y; if(y>maxY)maxY=y;
19577:         if(y>colMaxY[x]) colMaxY[x]=y;
19578:         if(x<bandCx) leftN++; else rightN++;
19579:       }
19580:       if(n<200) return [];
19581:       const meanL=sL/n,meanC=sC/n,bh=Math.max(1,maxY-minY),bw=Math.max(1,maxX-minX);
19582:       let dark=0;
19583:       for(let q=0,p=0;q<mask.length;q++,p+=4){ if(mask[q]<0.5) continue; if((d[p]+d[p+1]+d[p+2])/3<meanL*0.6) dark++; }
19584:       const F=[];

19651:   // Pixel-level Bio-Blend overlay for intraoral contraster shots. BIDIRECTIONAL:
19652:   //   bb > 0 => naturalization microstructure: マメロン / 周波条 / ハロー効果
19653:   //   bb < 0 => SMOOTH: minimise surface texture / anatomy, emphasise a smooth even surface
19654:   // ox = 2d context of the source image (used to build the enamel mask).
19655:   // ctx = output 2d context to draw effects onto.
19656:   // bb = signed bio-blend strength, -1..+1.
19657:   function applyIntraoralBioBlend(ctx, ox, w, h, bb){
19658:     if(Math.abs(bb) <= 0.01) return;
19659:     const smooth = bb < 0;
19660:     const amt = Math.abs(bb);   // 0..1
19661:     const em = enamelMaskCanvas(ox, w, h);
19662:     if(!em) return;
19663:     const emx = em.getContext('2d', {willReadFrequently: true});
19664:     const emD = emx.getImageData(0, 0, w, h).data;
19665:     let id; try{ id = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, w, h); } catch(e){ return; }
19666:     const d = id.data;
19667:     // For the SMOOTH side we blend each enamel pixel toward a blurred copy of itself, which
19668:     // evens out perikymata / mammellons / micro-texture and emphasises a smooth surface.
19669:     let blurD=null;
19670:     if(smooth){
19671:       try{
19672:         const bc=document.createElement('canvas'); bc.width=w; bc.height=h;
19673:         const bx=bc.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
19674:         bx.filter='blur('+Math.max(1,Math.round(Math.min(w,h)*0.004*(0.5+amt)))+'px)';
19675:         bx.drawImage(ctx.canvas,0,0); bx.filter='none';
19676:         blurD=bx.getImageData(0,0,w,h).data;
19677:       }catch(e){ blurD=null; }
19678:     }
19679:     // find incisal (top) and cervical (bottom) extent of the enamel band
19680:     let minY = h, maxY = 0;
19681:     for(let i = 0; i < emD.length; i += 4){
19682:       if(emD[i] > 64){
19683:         const py = ((i >> 2) / w) | 0;
19684:         if(py < minY) minY = py;
19685:         if(py > maxY) maxY = py;
19686:       }
19687:     }
19688:     if(minY >= maxY) return;
19689:     const enamelH = maxY - minY;
19690:     for(let y = minY; y <= maxY; y++){
19691:       const relY = (y - minY) / enamelH; // 0 = incisal edge, 1 = gingival
19692:       for(let x = 0; x < w; x++){
19693:         const qi = y * w + x;
19694:         const m = emD[qi << 2] / 255; // enamel mask weight at this pixel
19695:         if(m < 0.04) continue;
19696:         const p = qi << 2;
19697:         let r = d[p], g = d[p+1], b = d[p+2];
19698:         if(!smooth){
19699:           // ===== Naturalization (right side) =====
19700:           // ハロー効果 — incisal translucency: blue-grey shift in top 12% of enamel band
19701:           if(relY < 0.12){
19702:             const t = Math.pow(1 - relY / 0.12, 1.6);
19703:             const halo = t * amt * 0.20 * m;
19704:             const lum = (r + g + b) / 3;
19705:             r = clamp(r + (lum * 0.80 - r) * halo, 0, 255);
19706:             g = clamp(g + (lum * 0.90 - g) * halo, 0, 255);
19707:             b = clamp(b + (Math.min(255, lum * 1.06 + 14) - b) * halo, 0, 255);
19708:           }
19709:           // 周波条 — fine horizontal perikymata ridges every 4 px, concentrated near incisal
19710:           const periW = Math.max(0, 1 - relY * 2.2) * amt * 0.030 * m;
19711:           if(periW > 0.001){
19712:             const periMod = Math.sin((y % 4) * Math.PI * 0.5) * periW;
19713:             r = clamp(r + periMod * 65, 0, 255);
19714:             g = clamp(g + periMod * 65, 0, 255);
19715:             b = clamp(b + periMod * 65, 0, 255);
19716:           }
19717:           // マメロン — 3 developmental lobe brightness variations at incisal third
19718:           if(relY < 0.28){
19719:             const mamZ = Math.pow(1 - relY / 0.28, 1.3);
19720:             const lobeAmp = Math.cos(x / w * Math.PI * 6) * 0.5 + 0.5;
19721:             const mamS = lobeAmp * mamZ * amt * 0.050 * m;
19722:             const lum = (r + g + b) / 3;
19723:             r = clamp(r + (lum + 5 - r) * mamS, 0, 255);
19724:             g = clamp(g + (lum + 5 - g) * mamS, 0, 255);
19725:             b = clamp(b + (lum + 9 - b) * mamS, 0, 255);
19726:           }

20607:   async function ensureIntraoralFlag(src){

20610:     // subtype) and lips (whether lips/perioral tissue are present — gates lip/smile rendering).

20614:         // defaults to NO lips so the generator never paints lips onto a retracted view; the
20615:         // upload popup / editable control lets the user opt a lip-framed focus shot back in.

20625:         resolveLips();
20626:         return src.intraoral;
20627:       }
20628:     }catch(e){}

20630:     if(typeof src.intraoralDark!=='boolean') src.intraoralDark=false;

20635:   // Produce the after-image: photoreal AI backend when enabled+configured, else the local

20670:   async function regenerate(){
20671:     if(regenBusy){ regenQueued=true; return; }
20672:     const src=state._simSource||pickSource();
20673:     if(!src) return;
20674:     regenBusy=true; setBusy(true);
20675:     try{
20676:       const url=await produceImage(src);
20677:       // Slider edits UPDATE the currently selected gallery slot in place (other variations
20678:       // stay intact), so re-editing never makes a previously generated image disappear.
20679:       const r=Object.assign({}, state.simResult, {
20680:         dataUrl:url, source:src.dataUrl, srcObj:src, sourceKind:sourceKind(src),
20681:         createdAt:new Date().toISOString(), settings:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.simV96))
20682:       });
20683:       if(Array.isArray(state.simResults) && state.simResults.length){ state.simResults[state.simResultIndex||0]=r; }
20684:       else { state.simResults=[r]; state.simResultIndex=0; }

21722:   var _start=MediaRecorder.prototype.start;
21723:   MediaRecorder.prototype.start=function(){
21724:     try{ if(window.cfWait) cfWait.show('動画を生成中', 0); }catch(e){}
21725:     var self=this;
21726:     var done=function(){
21727:       try{ if(window.cfWait) cfWait.hide(); }catch(e){}
21728:       self.removeEventListener('stop', done);
21729:       self.removeEventListener('error', done);
21730:     };
21731:     this.addEventListener('stop', done);
21732:     this.addEventListener('error', done);
21733:     return _start.apply(this, arguments);
21734:   };
21735: })();
21736: </script>

24608:   async function cropUpperAnteriorTeeth(primaryDataUrl){
24609:     var img = await loadImg(primaryDataUrl);
24610:     var w = img.naturalWidth||img.width, h = img.naturalHeight||img.height;
24611:     var cropRect = null;
24612: 
24613:     // Try MediaPipe inner lip ring for a precise crop
24614:     var pts = await getLipInnerRing(img, w, h);
24615:     if(pts){
24616:       var minX=1e9,maxX=-1e9,minY=1e9,maxY=-1e9;
24617:       pts.forEach(function(p){ minX=Math.min(minX,p.x); maxX=Math.max(maxX,p.x); minY=Math.min(minY,p.y); maxY=Math.max(maxY,p.y); });
24618:       var bW = maxX-minX, bH = maxY-minY;
24619:       // Extend up 80% of ring height for crowns + gingival tissue; stop ~10% below center for lower-lip clearance
24620:       var cx = minX - bW*0.18;
24621:       var cy = minY - bH*0.85;
24622:       var cw = bW * 1.36;
24623:       var ch = ((minY+maxY)/2 + bH*0.12) - cy;
24624:       cropRect = {
24625:         x: Math.max(0, Math.round(cx)),
24626:         y: Math.max(0, Math.round(cy)),
24627:         w: Math.min(w-Math.max(0,Math.round(cx)), Math.round(cw)),
24628:         h: Math.min(h-Math.max(0,Math.round(cy)), Math.round(ch))
24629:       };
24630:     }
24631: 
24632:     // Fallback: centre-upper quarter of frame where anterior teeth typically appear
24633:     if(!cropRect || cropRect.w < 20 || cropRect.h < 20){
24634:       cropRect = {
24635:         x: Math.round(w*0.15),
24636:         y: Math.round(h*0.38),
24637:         w: Math.round(w*0.70),
24638:         h: Math.round(h*0.24)
24639:       };
24640:     }
24641: 
24642:     var c = document.createElement('canvas');
24643:     c.width = cropRect.w; c.height = cropRect.h;
24644:     var ctx = c.getContext('2d');
24645:     ctx.drawImage(img, cropRect.x, cropRect.y, cropRect.w, cropRect.h, 0, 0, cropRect.w, cropRect.h);
24646:     return { dataUrl: c.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.93), cropRect: cropRect, imgW:w, imgH:h };
24647:   }
24648:   window.cfCropUpperAnteriorTeeth = cropUpperAnteriorTeeth;

24650:   // Generate the refined image. FACIAL: full-frame, identity-preserving, LOW denoising
24651:   // (a gentle balance pass — never an intraoral close-up). FOCUS: dental crop, intraoral,
24652:   // a little more latitude. Golden-proportion change is clamped via low denoising_strength.
24653:   async function generateRefinedViaAI(imageDataUrl, imageType){
24654:     var epRaw = getAiEndpoint();
24655:     if(!epRaw){ throw new Error('AI バックエンドが設定されていません'); }
24656:     var _refineLedgerId=null;
24657:     if(typeof window.cfConsumeToken==='function'){
24658:       var tk = await window.cfConsumeToken('generation');
24659:       if(tk && tk.ok===false){
24660:         if(tk.reason==='insufficient' && typeof window.cfShowTokenEmpty==='function') window.cfShowTokenEmpty();
24661:         var err = new Error(tk.reason==='insufficient' ? 'トークンが不足しています' : 'トークンの確認に失敗しました');
24662:         err.cfNoTokens = true; throw err;
24663:       }
24664:       _refineLedgerId=(tk&&tk.ledgerId)||null;
24665:     }
24666:     var ep = epRaw.replace(/\/+$/,'');
24667:     if(!/\/simulate$/.test(ep)) ep += '/simulate';
24668:     var basePayload = {};
24669:     if(typeof window.buildSimulationPayload==='function' && window.state && window.state.simResult && window.state.simResult.srcObj){
24670:       try{ basePayload = window.buildSimulationPayload(window.state.simResult.srcObj); }catch(e){}
24671:     }
24672:     var facial = (imageType==='facial');
24673:     var payload = Object.assign({}, basePayload, {
24674:       image: imageDataUrl,
24675:       prompt: buildGoldenProportionPrompt(imageType) + (typeof window.cfSmileDesignerPromptFragment==='function' ? ' ' + window.cfSmileDesignerPromptFragment(imageType) : ''),
24676:       concept: 'prosthetic',
24677:       // CLAMP: facial gets a much smaller denoise so the pass stays a gentle balance,
24678:       // not a re-generation that can drift identity / over-whiten.
24679:       denoising_strength: facial ? 0.26 : 0.42,
24680:       control_weight: facial ? 0.85 : 0.72,
24681:       // FACIAL is NEVER intraoral and keeps the lips — that focus-mode flag is exactly
24682:       // what produced the "focus-style local replacement on a facial photo" breakage.
24683:       intraoral: !facial,
24684:       lips: facial,
24685:       extraction_mode: false,
24686:       hybrid_mode: false,
24687:       negative_prompt: [
24688:         'blurry, low quality, artifacts, watermark, text, oversaturated,',
24689:         'pure white teeth, flat teeth, fused teeth, generic veneers, denture look,',
24690:         'unnatural color, brackets, braces, wires, metal, discoloration, stains,',
24691:         'uneven gingiva, misaligned gingival zenith, gaps, crowding, dark shadows,',
24692:         'changed face, changed lips, changed skin, deformed teeth, cartoon, painting, sketch, anime'
24693:       ].join(' ')
24694:     });
24695:     try{
24696:       var res = await fetchWithRetry(ep, JSON.stringify(payload));
24697:       var j = await res.json();
24698:       if(j && j.error) throw new Error(String(j.error).slice(0,300));
24699:       var out = j.image || j.dataUrl || j.output || (Array.isArray(j.images) ? j.images[0] : null) || null;
24700:       if(!out) throw new Error('二次生成で画像が返りませんでした');
24701:       return out;
24702:     }catch(refErr){
24703:       // 生成失敗 → トークン返却
24704:       if(_refineLedgerId!=null && typeof window.cfRefundGeneration==='function'){
24705:         try{ window.cfRefundGeneration(_refineLedgerId); }catch(e){ console.error('[CF Gate] refine refund failed',e); }
24706:       }
24707:       throw refErr;
24708:     }
24709:   }

24711:   function toneMatchSimple(refCtx, srcCtx, w, h, str){
24712:     // Fast global mean-channel shift: match refinedCrop colour to primary at crop region
24713:     try{
24714:       var rd = refCtx.getImageData(0,0,w,h).data;
24715:       var sd = srcCtx.getImageData(0,0,w,h).data;
24716:       var rR=0,rG=0,rB=0, sR=0,sG=0,sB=0, n=0;
24717:       for(var i=3; i<rd.length; i+=4){
24718:         if(rd[i]>20 && sd[i]>20){
24719:           rR+=rd[i-3]; rG+=rd[i-2]; rB+=rd[i-1];
24720:           sR+=sd[i-3]; sG+=sd[i-2]; sB+=sd[i-1];
24721:           n++;
24722:         }
24723:       }
24724:       if(!n) return;
24725:       var dR=(sR-rR)/n*str, dG=(sG-rG)/n*str, dB=(sB-rB)/n*str;
24726:       var id=refCtx.getImageData(0,0,w,h);
24727:       var d=id.data;
24728:       for(var i=3; i<d.length; i+=4){
24729:         if(d[i]>20){
24730:           d[i-3]=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,(d[i-3]+dR)|0));
24731:           d[i-2]=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,(d[i-2]+dG)|0));
24732:           d[i-1]=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,(d[i-1]+dB)|0));
24733:         }
24734:       }
24735:       refCtx.putImageData(id,0,0);
24736:     }catch(e){}
24737:   }

24679:       denoising_strength: facial ? 0.26 : 0.42,
24680:       control_weight: facial ? 0.85 : 0.72,
24681:       // FACIAL is NEVER intraoral and keeps the lips — that focus-mode flag is exactly
24682:       // what produced the "focus-style local replacement on a facial photo" breakage.
24683:       intraoral: !facial,
24684:       lips: facial,
24685:       extraction_mode: false,
24686:       hybrid_mode: false,
24687:       negative_prompt: [
24688:         'blurry, low quality, artifacts, watermark, text, oversaturated,',
24689:         'pure white teeth, flat teeth, fused teeth, generic veneers, denture look,',
24690:         'unnatural color, brackets, braces, wires, metal, discoloration, stains,',
24691:         'uneven gingiva, misaligned gingival zenith, gaps, crowding, dark shadows,',
24692:         'changed face, changed lips, changed skin, deformed teeth, cartoon, painting, sketch, anime'
24693:       ].join(' ')
24694:     });
24695:     try{
24696:       var res = await fetchWithRetry(ep, JSON.stringify(payload));
24697:       var j = await res.json();
24698:       if(j && j.error) throw new Error(String(j.error).slice(0,300));
24699:       var out = j.image || j.dataUrl || j.output || (Array.isArray(j.images) ? j.images[0] : null) || null;
24700:       if(!out) throw new Error('二次生成で画像が返りませんでした');
24701:       return out;
24702:     }catch(refErr){
24703:       // 生成失敗 → トークン返却
24704:       if(_refineLedgerId!=null && typeof window.cfRefundGeneration==='function'){
24705:         try{ window.cfRefundGeneration(_refineLedgerId); }catch(e){ console.error('[CF Gate] refine refund failed',e); }
24706:       }
24707:       throw refErr;
24708:     }
24709:   }

24711:   function toneMatchSimple(refCtx, srcCtx, w, h, str){
24712:     // Fast global mean-channel shift: match refinedCrop colour to primary at crop region
24713:     try{
24714:       var rd = refCtx.getImageData(0,0,w,h).data;
24715:       var sd = srcCtx.getImageData(0,0,w,h).data;
24716:       var rR=0,rG=0,rB=0, sR=0,sG=0,sB=0, n=0;
24717:       for(var i=3; i<rd.length; i+=4){
24718:         if(rd[i]>20 && sd[i]>20){
24719:           rR+=rd[i-3]; rG+=rd[i-2]; rB+=rd[i-1];
24720:           sR+=sd[i-3]; sG+=sd[i-2]; sB+=sd[i-1];
24721:           n++;
24722:         }
24723:       }
24724:       if(!n) return;
24725:       var dR=(sR-rR)/n*str, dG=(sG-rG)/n*str, dB=(sB-rB)/n*str;
24726:       var id=refCtx.getImageData(0,0,w,h);
24727:       var d=id.data;
24728:       for(var i=3; i<d.length; i+=4){
24729:         if(d[i]>20){
24730:           d[i-3]=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,(d[i-3]+dR)|0));
24731:           d[i-2]=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,(d[i-2]+dG)|0));
24732:           d[i-1]=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,(d[i-1]+dB)|0));
24733:         }
24734:       }
24735:       refCtx.putImageData(id,0,0);
24736:     }catch(e){}
24737:   }

24739:   // Local fallback matte builder (convex-ish feathered polygon) if the shared engine matte
24740:   // isn't available. NEVER a rectangle — always the lip/region contour, heavily feathered.
24741:   function buildPolygonMatteFallback(poly, w, h, expand, featherPx){
24742:     var cx=0,cy=0; poly.forEach(function(p){cx+=p.x;cy+=p.y;}); cx/=poly.length; cy/=poly.length;
24743:     var m1=document.createElement('canvas'); m1.width=w; m1.height=h;
24744:     var m1x=m1.getContext('2d'); m1x.fillStyle='#fff'; m1x.beginPath();
24745:     poly.forEach(function(p,i){ var x=cx+(p.x-cx)*expand, y=cy+(p.y-cy)*expand; i?m1x.lineTo(x,y):m1x.moveTo(x,y); });
24746:     m1x.closePath(); m1x.fill();
24747:     var mk=document.createElement('canvas'); mk.width=w; mk.height=h;
24748:     var mx=mk.getContext('2d'); mx.filter='blur('+Math.max(6,Math.round(featherPx))+'px)'; mx.drawImage(m1,0,0); mx.filter='none';
24749:     return mk;
24750:   }
24751: 
24752:   // Luminance stats over pixels whose matte alpha is within [aLo,aHi] (0..255).
24753:   function regionLumStats(px, ma, aLo, aHi){
24754:     var sum=0, sum2=0, n=0;
24755:     for(var i=0;i<px.length;i+=4){
24756:       var a=ma[i+3]!==undefined?ma[i+3]:ma[i]; // matte drawn white→ use any channel
24757:       if(a<aLo||a>aHi) continue;
24758:       var l=(px[i]*0.299+px[i+1]*0.587+px[i+2]*0.114);
24759:       sum+=l; sum2+=l*l; n++;
24760:     }
24761:     if(!n) return {mean:0,variance:0,n:0};
24762:     var mean=sum/n; return {mean:mean, variance:Math.max(0,sum2/n-mean*mean), n:n};
24763:   }

24765:   // Composite the refined FULL image over the primary using a soft, contour-based matte.
24766:   // Returns { dataUrl, metrics }. metrics drives accept/reject (no rectangle anywhere).
24767:   async function compositeRefinedSoft(primaryDataUrl, refinedFullUrl, imageType, poly){
24768:     var base = await loadImg(primaryDataUrl);
24769:     var refined = await loadImg(refinedFullUrl);

===== TARGET 24861 / LINES 24826-24955 =====
24826:       roiSupport:Math.round(rx*scales.support)+'x'+Math.round(ry*scales.support),
24827:       roiContext:Math.round(rx*scales.context)+'x'+Math.round(ry*scales.context) };
24828:     try{
24829:       var maD=matte.getContext('2d').getImageData(0,0,w,h).data;
24830:       var baD=primX.getImageData(0,0,w,h).data;
24831:       var reD=refX.getImageData(0,0,w,h).data;   // tone-matched refined
24832:       var rsD=ctx.getImageData(0,0,w,h).data;    // final result
24833:       // outsideRegionChangeScore: mean |Δlum| where matte≈0 (should be ~0)
24834:       var oS=0,oN=0, sS=0,sN=0;
24835:       for(var i=0;i<maD.length;i+=4){
24836:         var a=maD[i+3]; // matte weight is the ALPHA channel (RGB stays 255)
24837:         var lb=(baD[i]*0.299+baD[i+1]*0.587+baD[i+2]*0.114);

24842:           sS+=Math.abs(lref-lb); sN++;
24843:         }
24844:       }

24846:       metrics.seamScore = sN? +(sS/sN).toFixed(2) : 0;
24847:       // whiteness / flatness of the refined teeth in the CORE (alpha>170)
24848:       var core=regionLumStats(reD, maD, 170, 255);
24849:       metrics.coreMeanLum=+core.mean.toFixed(1); metrics.coreVariance=+core.variance.toFixed(1);
24850:       var whiteMeanThr = facial?224:236, whiteVarThr = facial?110:70;
24851:       var whiteFlat = core.n>50 && core.mean>whiteMeanThr && core.variance<whiteVarThr;
24852:       var outThr = facial?7:15, seamThr = facial?26:42;
24853:       metrics.artifactReject = !!(whiteFlat || metrics.outsideRegionChangeScore>outThr || metrics.seamScore>seamThr);
24854:       metrics.rejectCause = whiteFlat?'white-flat-teeth':(metrics.outsideRegionChangeScore>outThr?'outside-change':(metrics.seamScore>seamThr?'seam':''));
24855:     }catch(e){ metrics.artifactReject=false; metrics.metricError=String(e&&e.message||e); }
24856: 
24857:     return { dataUrl: c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',0.93), metrics: metrics };
24858:   }

24875:   // Gated refinement: 1) evaluate ROI/landmarks/imageType → 2) decide if a refine is safe →
24876:   // 3) run a CLAMPED full-frame pass → 4) soft-matte composite (no rectangle) →
24877:   // 5) quality check → 6) reject & keep the first-pass on any artifact, else adopt.
24878:   window.runSecondaryRefinement = async function(){
24879:     var btn = document.getElementById('cfRefineBtn');
24880:     var result = window.state && window.state.simResult;
24881:     var dbg = { imageType:null, secondaryRefineEnabled:false, secondaryRefineSkippedReason:null,
24882:       roiCore:null, roiSupport:null, roiContext:null, blendMode:null, seamScore:null,
24883:       outsideRegionChangeScore:null, artifactReject:null, finalSource:'first-pass' };
24884: 
24885:     if(!result || !result.dataUrl){ toast('生成結果が見つかりません'); return; }
24886:     if(!getAiEndpoint()){ toast('AI バックエンドが設定されていません'); return; }
24887: 
24888:     var imageType = (result.sourceKind==='Facial') ? 'facial' : 'focus';
24889:     dbg.imageType = imageType;
24890: 
24891:     if(btn){ btn.disabled=true; btn.innerHTML='…<br>処理中'; }
24892:     try{
24893:       var primaryDataUrl = result._primaryDataUrl || result.dataUrl;
24894:       var primImg = await loadImg(primaryDataUrl);
24895:       var w = primImg.naturalWidth||primImg.width, h = primImg.naturalHeight||primImg.height;
24896: 
24897:       // ── GATE: facial needs reliable mouth landmarks; without them, a safe local refine
24898:       //    is impossible → SKIP and keep the first-pass (never force a blind patch). ──
24899:       var poly = null;
24900:       if(imageType==='facial'){
24901:         poly = await getLipOuterRing(primImg, w, h);
24902:         if(!poly){
24903:           dbg.secondaryRefineSkippedReason='no_facial_landmarks';
24904:           console.log('[CaseFlow Refine]', dbg);
24905:           if(btn){ btn.disabled=false; btn.innerHTML='✦ Refine<br>精密生成'; btn.style.opacity=''; }
24906:           toast('顔のランドマークが安定しないため二次生成をスキップしました（一次結果を維持）');
24907:           return;
24908:         }
24909:       }
24910:       dbg.secondaryRefineEnabled = true;
24911: 
24912:       // ── CLAMPED full-frame refine pass (facial = identity-preserving, low denoise) ──
24913:       toast(imageType==='facial' ? 'スマイルバランスを control 補正中…' : 'ゴールデンプロポーション二次生成中…');
24914:       var refinedFull = await generateRefinedViaAI(primaryDataUrl, imageType);
24915: 
24916:       // ── Soft-matte composite (polygon/ellipse, never a rectangle) + quality metrics ──
24917:       toast('一次結果へなじませ中…');
24918:       var comp = await compositeRefinedSoft(primaryDataUrl, refinedFull, imageType, poly);
24919:       var m = comp.metrics||{};
24920:       dbg.roiCore=m.roiCore; dbg.roiSupport=m.roiSupport; dbg.roiContext=m.roiContext;
24921:       dbg.blendMode=m.blendMode; dbg.seamScore=m.seamScore;
24922:       dbg.outsideRegionChangeScore=m.outsideRegionChangeScore; dbg.artifactReject=!!m.artifactReject;
24923: 
24924:       if(m.artifactReject){
24925:         // ── FALLBACK: a worse/patchy result is never adopted — keep the first-pass. ──
24926:         dbg.finalSource='first-pass';
24927:         dbg.secondaryRefineSkippedReason='artifact_reject:'+(m.rejectCause||'unknown');
24928:         console.log('[CaseFlow Refine]', dbg, m);
24929:         if(btn){ btn.disabled=false; btn.innerHTML='✦ Refine<br>精密生成'; btn.style.opacity=''; }
24930:         toast('二次生成が不自然なため一次結果を維持しました');
24931:         return;
24932:       }
24933: 
24934:       // ── ADOPT the second-pass ──
24935:       dbg.finalSource='second-pass';
24936:       result._primaryDataUrl = result._primaryDataUrl || result.dataUrl;
24937:       result._secondaryRefined = true;
24938:       result.dataUrl = comp.dataUrl;

24940:         var idx = window.state.simResultIndex;
24941:         if(window.state.simResults[idx]) window.state.simResults[idx] = result;
24942:       }

24944:       if(typeof window.renderResult==='function') window.renderResult();
24945:       if(btn){ btn.disabled=true; btn.innerHTML='✦ Refined<br>精密済み'; btn.style.opacity='0.65'; btn.title='二次生成完了 — 精密済み'; }

24861:     var btn = document.getElementById('cfRefineBtn');
24862:     if(!btn) return;
24863:     var result = window.state && window.state.simResult;
24864:     var isFacial = result && result.sourceKind === 'Facial';

24875:   // Gated refinement: 1) evaluate ROI/landmarks/imageType → 2) decide if a refine is safe →
24876:   // 3) run a CLAMPED full-frame pass → 4) soft-matte composite (no rectangle) →
24877:   // 5) quality check → 6) reject & keep the first-pass on any artifact, else adopt.
24878:   window.runSecondaryRefinement = async function(){
24879:     var btn = document.getElementById('cfRefineBtn');
24880:     var result = window.state && window.state.simResult;
24881:     var dbg = { imageType:null, secondaryRefineEnabled:false, secondaryRefineSkippedReason:null,
24882:       roiCore:null, roiSupport:null, roiContext:null, blendMode:null, seamScore:null,
24883:       outsideRegionChangeScore:null, artifactReject:null, finalSource:'first-pass' };
24884: 
24885:     if(!result || !result.dataUrl){ toast('生成結果が見つかりません'); return; }
24886:     if(!getAiEndpoint()){ toast('AI バックエンドが設定されていません'); return; }
24887: 
24888:     var imageType = (result.sourceKind==='Facial') ? 'facial' : 'focus';
24889:     dbg.imageType = imageType;
24890: 
24891:     if(btn){ btn.disabled=true; btn.innerHTML='…<br>処理中'; }
24892:     try{
24893:       var primaryDataUrl = result._primaryDataUrl || result.dataUrl;
24894:       var primImg = await loadImg(primaryDataUrl);
24895:       var w = primImg.naturalWidth||primImg.width, h = primImg.naturalHeight||primImg.height;
24896: 
24897:       // ── GATE: facial needs reliable mouth landmarks; without them, a safe local refine
24898:       //    is impossible → SKIP and keep the first-pass (never force a blind patch). ──
24899:       var poly = null;
24900:       if(imageType==='facial'){
24901:         poly = await getLipOuterRing(primImg, w, h);
24902:         if(!poly){
24903:           dbg.secondaryRefineSkippedReason='no_facial_landmarks';
24904:           console.log('[CaseFlow Refine]', dbg);
24905:           if(btn){ btn.disabled=false; btn.innerHTML='✦ Refine<br>精密生成'; btn.style.opacity=''; }
24906:           toast('顔のランドマークが安定しないため二次生成をスキップしました（一次結果を維持）');
24907:           return;
24908:         }
24909:       }
24910:       dbg.secondaryRefineEnabled = true;
24911: 
24912:       // ── CLAMPED full-frame refine pass (facial = identity-preserving, low denoise) ──
24913:       toast(imageType==='facial' ? 'スマイルバランスを control 補正中…' : 'ゴールデンプロポーション二次生成中…');
24914:       var refinedFull = await generateRefinedViaAI(primaryDataUrl, imageType);
24915: 
24916:       // ── Soft-matte composite (polygon/ellipse, never a rectangle) + quality metrics ──
24917:       toast('一次結果へなじませ中…');
24918:       var comp = await compositeRefinedSoft(primaryDataUrl, refinedFull, imageType, poly);
24919:       var m = comp.metrics||{};
24920:       dbg.roiCore=m.roiCore; dbg.roiSupport=m.roiSupport; dbg.roiContext=m.roiContext;
24921:       dbg.blendMode=m.blendMode; dbg.seamScore=m.seamScore;
24922:       dbg.outsideRegionChangeScore=m.outsideRegionChangeScore; dbg.artifactReject=!!m.artifactReject;
24923: 
24924:       if(m.artifactReject){
24925:         // ── FALLBACK: a worse/patchy result is never adopted — keep the first-pass. ──
24926:         dbg.finalSource='first-pass';
24927:         dbg.secondaryRefineSkippedReason='artifact_reject:'+(m.rejectCause||'unknown');
24928:         console.log('[CaseFlow Refine]', dbg, m);
24929:         if(btn){ btn.disabled=false; btn.innerHTML='✦ Refine<br>精密生成'; btn.style.opacity=''; }
24930:         toast('二次生成が不自然なため一次結果を維持しました');
24931:         return;
24932:       }
24933: 
24934:       // ── ADOPT the second-pass ──
24935:       dbg.finalSource='second-pass';
24936:       result._primaryDataUrl = result._primaryDataUrl || result.dataUrl;
24937:       result._secondaryRefined = true;
24938:       result.dataUrl = comp.dataUrl;
24939:       if(Array.isArray(window.state.simResults) && typeof window.state.simResultIndex==='number'){
24940:         var idx = window.state.simResultIndex;
24941:         if(window.state.simResults[idx]) window.state.simResults[idx] = result;
24942:       }
24943:       console.log('[CaseFlow Refine]', dbg, m);
24944:       if(typeof window.renderResult==='function') window.renderResult();
24945:       if(btn){ btn.disabled=true; btn.innerHTML='✦ Refined<br>精密済み'; btn.style.opacity='0.65'; btn.title='二次生成完了 — 精密済み'; }
24946:       toast('✦ 二次生成完了 — バランス補正を適用');
24947:     }catch(e){
24948:       console.error('[CaseFlow Refine] Secondary refinement failed', e, dbg);
24949:       if(btn){ btn.disabled=false; btn.innerHTML='✦ Refine<br>精密生成'; btn.style.opacity=''; }
24950:       var msg = e.cfNoTokens ? e.message : ('二次生成エラー: '+(e.message||String(e)).slice(0,120));
24951:       toast(msg);
24952:     }
24953:   };
